Hence as Witches doe ſtrange and ſupernatural workes, 
and truely vnto reaſon worthy of wonder; ſo the Impoſtox

And hence it commeth to paſſe, that with vndiſcerning

for another, 

From hence it is alſo neceſſarily concluaed, that as Witch-

Thoſe things which are manifeſt vnto thePhylicion alone,

This concluſion concerning the infallibilitie of a ſuper-

world,

a manifeft tranſcending power , manner, or motion in any 
{uppoſed diſeaſe, there is an yndoubted concluſion of the 
Author. Where likewiſe remedics finde concomitances, or
